## R3 closure applied in 10f0c1e

- **D5-R3-01 closure** (Blocked prepare tokens are still later retired on successful supersede):
  - renderer/js/r3-0d-engineer-orchestrator.js `_maybePublishBlocked`: do NOT assign
    `_currentToken` from a blocked prepare. Only successful publishes (Step 5) own the
    active token. Subscriber snapshots still surface the blocked attempt's token for
    diagnostic visibility, but `_currentToken` stays untouched.

## Regression coverage (Section O)

- O1a-e — forged prepare(A) → valid prepare(B) → retry prepare(A): A is accepted (not
  mis-retired) and overwrites B.
- O2a-c — valid publish(A) → forged prepare(B) (blocked) → same-token publish(A) is
  accepted; the blocked B did not retire A.

## Local verification on 10f0c1e

   130	  function _notifySubscribers(snapshot) {
   131	    // Use a snapshot copy of the subscribers array so a listener that subscribes/unsubscribes
   132	    // during notification does NOT corrupt iteration.
   133	    var listeners = HI.safeArraySlice(_subscribers);
   134	    for (var i = 0; i < listeners.length; i++) {
   135	      try { listeners[i](snapshot); } catch (e) { /* fail-closed; never let a listener throw out */ }
   136	    }
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  // ---------- Public API: prepareEngineerInsight ------------------------------------------------
   140	  /**
   141	   * prepareEngineerInsight({ hypothesisSet, prioritySet, generationToken }, opts) —
   142	   *   1. Bump internal sequence + capture local snapshot ID.
   143	   *   2. Build D5 brief envelope.
   144	   *   3. Build D5 viewmodel.
   145	   *   4. If still latest (no invalidation occurred mid-build), publish + notify.
   146	   *   5. Return the result either way (blocked or success).
   147	   */
   148	  function prepareEngineerInsight(inputIn, optsIn) {
   149	    // Codex D5 R1-01 closure: bump _internalSeq at the START of every prepare call so any
   150	    // concurrent / replayed / out-of-order prepare result whose seqAtEntry no longer matches
   151	    // _internalSeq is dropped at the publish gate (Step 4 below). Without this, a later
   152	    // prepare(token=B) could be observed BEFORE an earlier prepare(token=A) and the earlier
   153	    // result would still publish, overwriting the newer state. With the per-call bump, the
   154	    // earlier call's seqAtEntry < _internalSeq at publish time → dropped.
   155	    _internalSeq += 1;
   156	    var seqAtEntry = _internalSeq;
   157	
   158	    // ---- Step 1 — basic input shape ----------------------------------------------------------
   159	    if (!_isPlainObject(inputIn)) {
   160	      var blocked = VM.buildBlockedViewModel('error-sanitized', [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   161	      _maybePublishBlocked(blocked, null, null, seqAtEntry);
   162	      return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: false, viewModel: blocked, displayState: 'error-sanitized' });
   163	    }
   164	    var allowedKeys = ['hypothesisSet', 'prioritySet', 'generationToken'];
   165	    var keys = HI.safeOwnKeys(inputIn);
   166	    for (var ki = 0; ki < keys.length; ki++) {
   167	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(allowedKeys, keys[ki]) === -1) {
   168	        var blockedKey = VM.buildBlockedViewModel('error-sanitized', [CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   169	        _maybePublishBlocked(blockedKey, null, null, seqAtEntry);
   170	        return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: false, viewModel: blockedKey, displayState: 'error-sanitized' });
   171	      }
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    var hypothesisSet = inputIn.hypothesisSet;
   175	    var prioritySet = inputIn.prioritySet;
   176	    var generationToken = inputIn.generationToken;
   177	
   178	    if (typeof generationToken !== 'string' || generationToken.length === 0) {
   179	      var blockedTok = VM.buildBlockedViewModel('error-sanitized', [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   180	      _maybePublishBlocked(blockedTok, null, null, seqAtEntry);
   181	      return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: false, viewModel: blockedTok, displayState: 'error-sanitized' });
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    // ---- Codex D5 R2-01 closure: retired-token replay rejection -----------------------------
   185	    // A retired token is one that was previously the active token and has since been
   186	    // superseded by a newer successful publish OR by invalidate(). Re-using it would
   187	    // overwrite the current state with stale content. Drop with stale-cleared.
   188	    if (HI.safeHasOwn(_retiredTokens, generationToken)) {
   189	      var blockedReplay = VM.buildBlockedViewModel('stale-cleared', [CODES.STALE_EVIDENCE]);
   190	      // Do NOT mutate _currentEnvelope / _currentViewModel — the legitimate current state
   191	      // (which carries a NEWER token) must remain visible. The blocked result is returned
   192	      // to the caller so they can log it, but the orchestrator does NOT publish over the
   193	      // active state.
   194	      return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: false, viewModel: blockedReplay, displayState: 'stale-cleared' });
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    // ---- Step 2 — build D5 envelope (handles its own authority gates) -----------------------
   198	    var ebInput = HI.deepFreeze({ hypothesisSet: hypothesisSet, prioritySet: prioritySet });
   199	    var ebOpts = optsIn === undefined ? undefined : optsIn;
   200	    var ebResult = EB.buildEngineerBrief(ebInput, ebOpts);
   201	
   202	    if (!(ebResult && ebResult.valid === true)) {
   203	      // Brief build blocked → blocked viewmodel, no publication of an authoritative state.
   204	      var blockedEb = VM.buildBlockedViewModel('blocked',
   205	        (ebResult && HI.safeIsArray(ebResult.reasonCodes)) ? ebResult.reasonCodes : [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED]);
   206	      _maybePublishBlocked(blockedEb, _associationFromInputs(hypothesisSet, prioritySet), generationToken, seqAtEntry);
   207	      return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: false, viewModel: blockedEb, displayState: 'blocked' });
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    // ---- Step 3 — build viewmodel ------------------------------------------------------------
   211	    var vmResult = VM.buildEngineerViewModel(ebResult.engineerBrief, undefined);
   212	    if (!(vmResult && vmResult.valid === true)) {
   213	      var blockedVm = VM.buildBlockedViewModel('error-sanitized', [CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION]);
   214	      _maybePublishBlocked(blockedVm, _associationFromInputs(hypothesisSet, prioritySet), generationToken, seqAtEntry);
   215	      return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: false, viewModel: blockedVm, displayState: 'error-sanitized' });
   216	    }
   217	
   218	    // ---- Step 4 — staleness check ------------------------------------------------------------
   219	    // If _internalSeq has advanced since we entered (e.g., invalidate() was called in between),
   220	    // the build result is STALE — drop it on the floor. The caller still receives the result
   221	    // (so they can log it locally), but the orchestrator state stays cleared.
   222	    if (_internalSeq !== seqAtEntry) {
   223	      // Stale — explicitly do NOT publish, do NOT notify subscribers.
   224	      return HI.deepFreeze({
   225	        valid: false,
   226	        viewModel: VM.buildBlockedViewModel('stale-cleared', [CODES.STALE_EVIDENCE]),
   227	        displayState: 'stale-cleared',
   228	      });
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    // ---- Step 5 — publish + notify -----------------------------------------------------------
   232	    // Codex D5 R2-01 closure: when a NEW token supersedes the previous _currentToken, retire
   233	    // the old token so a future replay cannot overwrite this newer publish.
   234	    if (_currentToken !== null && _currentToken !== generationToken) {
   235	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(_retiredTokens, _currentToken, true);
   236	    }
   237	    _currentEnvelope = ebResult.engineerBrief;
   238	    _currentViewModel = vmResult;
   239	    _currentToken = generationToken;
   240	    _currentCase = HI.deepFreeze({
   241	      caseId: ebResult.engineerBrief.brief.identity.caseId,
   242	      sessionId: ebResult.engineerBrief.brief.identity.sessionId,
   243	      lapId: ebResult.engineerBrief.brief.identity.lapId,
   244	    });
   245	
   246	    var snapshot = _buildSubscriberSnapshot(vmResult, _currentCase, generationToken);
   247	    _notifySubscribers(snapshot);
   248	
   249	    return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: true, viewModel: vmResult.viewModel, displayState: vmResult.viewModel.displayState });
   250	  }
   251	
   252	  // ---------- Public API: currentState ----------------------------------------------------------
   253	  /**
   254	   * currentState() — returns a snapshot of the published viewmodel state, or null if nothing
   255	   * is published. The snapshot is a deep-frozen plain object — NOT the authoritative
   256	   * viewmodel (callers cannot use it to spoof producer attestation).
   257	   */
   258	  function currentState() {
   259	    if (_currentViewModel === null) return null;
   260	    return _buildSubscriberSnapshot(_currentViewModel, _currentCase, _currentToken);
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  // ---------- Public API: getCurrentAuthoritativeEnvelope ---------------------------------------
   264	  /**
   265	   * getCurrentAuthoritativeEnvelope() — returns the authoritative D5 envelope (or null).
   266	   * Used by the host wiring code that needs to pass the authoritative reference to
   267	   * downstream R3.0E / R3.0F consumers. The returned reference IS in the D5 authoritative
   268	   * WeakSet — caller MUST treat it as a sealed authority and NOT mutate / clone / forward
   269	   * to untrusted consumers.
   270	   */
   271	  function getCurrentAuthoritativeEnvelope() {
   272	    return _currentEnvelope;
   273	  }
   274	  function getCurrentAuthoritativeViewModel() {
   275	    return (_currentViewModel && _currentViewModel.valid === true) ? _currentViewModel.viewModel : null;
   276	  }
   277	
   278	  // ---------- Public API: invalidate ------------------------------------------------------------
   279	  /**
   280	   * invalidate(reasonCode, contextSnapshot?) — clears the published viewmodel + bumps the
   281	   * internal sequence so any in-flight stale callback is dropped. Notifies subscribers with
   282	   * a stale-cleared snapshot.
   283	   */
   284	  function invalidate(reasonCode, contextSnapshot) {
   285	    if (typeof reasonCode !== 'string'
   286	        || HI.safeArrayIndexOf(INVALIDATION_REASONS, reasonCode) === -1) {
   287	      // Unknown invalidation reason — still treat as a sanitized error to fail closed.
   288	      reasonCode = 'application_restart';
   289	    }
   290	    _internalSeq += 1;
   291	    // Codex D5 R2-01 closure: retire the current token on invalidate so a stale prepare
   292	    // (e.g., the host re-uses the previous token after a case/session switch) cannot
   293	    // overwrite the cleared state.
   294	    if (_currentToken !== null) {
   295	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(_retiredTokens, _currentToken, true);
   296	    }
   297	    // Drop the authoritative envelope (no producer-attestation reference survives across
   298	    // invalidation), but keep a non-authoritative stale-cleared viewmodel so the UI shell
   299	    // can render an explicit "cleared" state instead of going blank.
   300	    _currentEnvelope = null;
   301	    var staleVm = VM.buildBlockedViewModel('stale-cleared',
   302	      _mapInvalidationReasonToCode(reasonCode));
   303	    _currentViewModel = staleVm;
   304	    _currentToken = null;
   305	    _currentCase = _isPlainObject(contextSnapshot) ? _snapshotAssociation(contextSnapshot) : null;
   306	
   307	    var snap = _buildSubscriberSnapshot(staleVm, _currentCase, null);
   308	    _notifySubscribers(snap);
   309	  }
   310	
   311	  function _mapInvalidationReasonToCode(reason) {
   312	    if (reason === 'imported_summary_opened') return [CODES.STALE_EVIDENCE, CODES.LIMITATION_IMPORTED_SUMMARY];
   313	    if (reason === 'case_deleted' || reason === 'case_archived') return [CODES.STALE_EVIDENCE];
   314	    return [CODES.STALE_EVIDENCE];
   315	  }
   316	
   317	  // ---------- Public API: subscribe -------------------------------------------------------------
   318	  /**
   319	   * subscribe(listener) — registers a snapshot listener. Returns an unsubscribe function.
   320	   * The listener is called synchronously after publish / invalidate. It receives a
   321	   * deep-frozen plain-object snapshot — never the authoritative viewmodel.
   322	   *
   323	   * Listener errors are SWALLOWED (never propagate up). This prevents a misbehaving renderer
   324	   * from preventing other listeners from receiving updates.
   325	   */
   326	  function subscribe(listener) {
   327	    if (typeof listener !== 'function') {
   328	      return function () { /* noop unsubscribe */ };
   329	    }
   330	    HI.safeArrayPush(_subscribers, listener);
   331	    return function unsubscribe() {
   332	      var i = HI.safeArrayIndexOf(_subscribers, listener);
   333	      if (i !== -1) _subscribers.splice(i, 1);
   334	    };
   335	  }
   336	
   337	  // ---------- Internal helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------
   338	  function _associationFromInputs(hs, ps) {
   339	    // Best-effort: pull caseAssociation from the authoritative envelope if possible. Both hs
   340	    // and ps carry caseAssociation; we prefer ps (D4 is the most recent authoritative layer).
   341	    try {
   342	      if (ps && _isPlainObject(ps.caseAssociation)) {
   343	        return _snapshotAssociation(ps.caseAssociation);
   344	      }
   345	      if (hs && _isPlainObject(hs.caseAssociation)) {
   346	        return _snapshotAssociation(hs.caseAssociation);
   347	      }
   348	    } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
   349	    return null;
   350	  }
   351	
   352	  function _maybePublishBlocked(blockedVm, association, generationToken, seqAtEntry) {
   353	    if (_internalSeq !== seqAtEntry) {
   354	      // Stale — do not publish.
   355	      return;
   356	    }
   357	    // Blocked publication: clear authoritative envelope/viewmodel but expose the blocked
   358	    // displayState to subscribers. The UI can render a sanitized "unavailable" / "blocked"
   359	    // panel.
   360	    // Codex D5 R3-01 closure: do NOT assign `_currentToken` from a blocked prepare. Only
   361	    // SUCCESSFUL publishes (Step 5 of prepareEngineerInsight) own the active token. A
   362	    // blocked attempt must not poison `_currentToken`, because a later successful publish
   363	    // with a different token would then retire this blocked-attempt token, and a retry of
   364	    // the original token would be wrongly rejected as a replay. Leaving `_currentToken`
   365	    // untouched means: a blocked attempt does NOT supersede the prior valid token (if
   366	    // any), and does NOT itself get retired by a future supersede.
   367	    _currentEnvelope = null;
   368	    _currentViewModel = blockedVm;
   369	    _currentCase = association;
   370	    // The subscriber snapshot still surfaces the blocked attempt's token for diagnostic
   371	    // visibility — but `_currentToken` itself does not change.
   372	    var snap = _buildSubscriberSnapshot(blockedVm, association, (typeof generationToken === 'string') ? generationToken : null);
   373	    _notifySubscribers(snap);
   374	  }
   375	
   376	  // ---------- Test-only reset (gated; safe for production) -------------------------------------
   377	  // Production builds NEVER call this. The renderer wiring uses invalidate() for state
   378	  // changes. _resetForTests is exposed under the `__test` namespace so its mere existence
   379	  // does NOT pollute the production API surface — Codex audit pattern.
   380	  function _resetForTests() {
   381	    _internalSeq += 1;
   382	    _currentEnvelope = null;
   383	    _currentViewModel = null;
   384	    _currentToken = null;
   385	    _currentCase = null;
   386	    _subscribers = [];
   387	    // Codex D5 R2-01 closure: tests need a clean retired-tokens map between runs so an
   388	    // earlier test's token reuse doesn't leak into a later test. _resetForTests is the
   389	    // gated test-only entry point per the existing convention.
   390	    _retiredTokens = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   391	  }
   392	
   393	  // ---------- Public API ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   394	  var api = {
   395	    INVALIDATION_REASONS: INVALIDATION_REASONS,
   396	    prepareEngineerInsight: prepareEngineerInsight,
   397	    currentState: currentState,
   398	    getCurrentAuthoritativeEnvelope: getCurrentAuthoritativeEnvelope,
   399	    getCurrentAuthoritativeViewModel: getCurrentAuthoritativeViewModel,
   400	    invalidate: invalidate,
   401	    subscribe: subscribe,
   402	    __test: HI.deepFreeze({ resetForTests: _resetForTests }),
   403	  };
   404	  try { HI.deepFreeze(api); } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
   405	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
   406	    module.exports = api;
   407	  } else if (root) {
   408	    root.R3_0D_EngineerOrchestrator = api;
   409	  }
   410	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);

   267	  // ---------- ID generators (deterministic from D3+D4 IDs) -------------------------------------
   268	  function _hash32(s) {
   269	    var h = 0x811c9dc5;
   270	    for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
   271	      h ^= s.charCodeAt(i);
   272	      h = (h + ((h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24))) >>> 0;
   273	    }
   274	    return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
   275	  }
   276	  function _fnv2(s) {
   277	    var slen = (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length : 0;
   278	    return _hash32(s) + _hash32(s + '|' + slen);
   279	  }
   280	  function _briefId(hsId, psId) { return 'brief_' + _fnv2('brief|' + hsId + '|' + psId); }
   281	
   282	  // ---------- Brief composer ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   283	  /**
   284	   * buildEngineerBrief({hypothesisSet, prioritySet}, opts) — produces a deep-frozen
   285	   * EngineerBrief envelope.
   286	   *
   287	   * Authority order (matches D4 R5-01 verifier-first ordering):
   288	   *   1. Snapshot input descriptors (no [[Get]] reads of inner fields yet)
   289	   *   2. HE.verifyAuthoritativeHypothesisSet(hypothesisSet) — FIRST authority gate
   290	   *   3. PE.verifyAuthoritativePrioritySet(prioritySet) — SECOND authority gate
   291	   *   4. Cross-binding: prioritySet.sourceHypothesisSetId === hypothesisSet.hypothesisSetId
   292	   *   5. Identity bindings: caseAssociation + sessionAssociation match
   293	   *   6. Snapshot opts descriptors
   294	   *   7. Resolve freshness reference time (clock invoked AT MOST ONCE post-authority)
   295	   *   8. Freshness check
   296	   *   9. Compose brief (re-projection of D3 + D4 fields; contradictions visible)
   297	   *   10. Validate brief shape via EBC.validateEngineerBriefShape (D1 contract layer)
   298	   *   11. Build activation gate signal
   299	   *   12. Materialize + deep-freeze + register
   300	   */
   301	  function buildEngineerBrief(inputIn, optsIn) {
   302	    try {
   303	      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
   304	        return _buildIntrinsicTamperBlock('intrinsic-tampering detected at entry');
   305	      }
   306	
   307	      // ---- Step 1 — input descriptor snapshot --------------------------------------------------
   308	      var inSnapR = _snapshotInputDescriptors(inputIn);
   309	      if (inSnapR.valid !== true) {
   310	        return RC.buildBlockedResult(inSnapR.reasonCodes, { detail: inSnapR.detail });
   311	      }
   312	      var inputSnap = inSnapR.snapshot;
   313	
   314	      // ---- Step 2 — D3 PRODUCER AUTHORITY (first read) -----------------------------------------
   315	      // D4 R5-01 closure: verifier MUST be the first read on caller-supplied data. The verifier
   316	      // itself does NOT fire Proxy traps for non-members (WeakSet.has via captured Reflect.apply).
   317	      if (typeof HE.verifyAuthoritativeHypothesisSet !== 'function'
   318	          || HE.verifyAuthoritativeHypothesisSet(inputSnap.hypothesisSet) !== true) {
   319	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'D3 hypothesisSet authority verification failed' });
   320	      }
   321	      var hs = inputSnap.hypothesisSet;
   322	
   323	      // ---- Step 3 — D4 PRODUCER AUTHORITY ------------------------------------------------------
   324	      if (typeof PE.verifyAuthoritativePrioritySet !== 'function'
   325	          || PE.verifyAuthoritativePrioritySet(inputSnap.prioritySet) !== true) {
   326	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'D4 prioritySet authority verification failed' });
   327	      }
   328	      var ps = inputSnap.prioritySet;
   329	
   330	      // ---- Step 4 — cross-binding --------------------------------------------------------------
   331	      // The D4 PrioritySet MUST have been derived from THIS D3 HypothesisSet.
   332	      if (ps.sourceHypothesisSetId !== hs.hypothesisSetId) {
   333	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'prioritySet not derived from this hypothesisSet' });
   334	      }
   335	      if (ps.sourceGraphId !== hs.sourceGraphId) {
   336	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED], { detail: 'sourceGraphId mismatch between D3 and D4' });
   337	      }
   338	
   339	      // ---- Step 5 — identity binding -----------------------------------------------------------
   340	      var hsCA = hs.caseAssociation;
   341	      var psCA = ps.caseAssociation;
   342	      if (!_isPlainObject(hsCA) || !_isPlainObject(psCA)) {
   343	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID], { detail: 'caseAssociation shape invalid' });
   344	      }
   345	      if (hsCA.caseId !== psCA.caseId || hsCA.sessionId !== psCA.sessionId || hsCA.lapId !== psCA.lapId) {
   346	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID], { detail: 'caseAssociation mismatch between D3 and D4' });
   347	      }
   348	      var hsSA = hs.sessionAssociation;
   349	      var psSA = ps.sessionAssociation;
   350	      if (!_isPlainObject(hsSA) || !_isPlainObject(psSA)
   351	          || hsSA.sessionId !== psSA.sessionId) {
   352	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID], { detail: 'sessionAssociation mismatch between D3 and D4' });
   353	      }
   354	
   355	      // ---- Step 6 — opts descriptor snapshot ---------------------------------------------------
   356	      var optsSnapR = _snapshotOptsDescriptors(optsIn);
   357	      if (optsSnapR.valid !== true) {
   358	        return RC.buildBlockedResult(optsSnapR.reasonCodes, { detail: optsSnapR.detail });
   359	      }
   360	      var optsSnap = optsSnapR.snapshot;
   361	
   362	      // ---- Step 7 — resolve freshness reference time (clock at most once, post-authority) -----
   363	      var refNowMs = null;
   364	      var resolvedClockIso = null;
   365	      if (typeof optsSnap.referenceNowMs === 'number' && HI.safeNumberIsInteger(optsSnap.referenceNowMs) && optsSnap.referenceNowMs >= 0) {
   366	        refNowMs = optsSnap.referenceNowMs;
   367	      } else if (typeof optsSnap.clock === 'function') {
   368	        var clockIso = null;
   369	        try { clockIso = optsSnap.clock(); } catch (eClock) { clockIso = null; }
   370	        if (typeof clockIso === 'string' && clockIso.length > 0) {
   371	          refNowMs = _isoToMs(clockIso);
   372	          if (typeof refNowMs === 'number') resolvedClockIso = clockIso;
   373	        }
   374	      }
   375	      if (typeof refNowMs !== 'number' || refNowMs !== refNowMs) {
   376	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.FRESHNESS_REFERENCE_MISSING], { detail: 'unable to resolve reference time' });
   377	      }
   378	      var maxAgeMs = (typeof optsSnap.maxAgeMs === 'number') ? optsSnap.maxAgeMs : DEFAULT_MAX_AGE_MS;
   379	
   380	      // ---- Step 8 — freshness check (uses hs.createdAt + ps.createdAt) ------------------------
   381	      var hsCreatedMs = (typeof hs.createdAt === 'string') ? _isoToMs(hs.createdAt) : null;
   382	      var psCreatedMs = (typeof ps.createdAt === 'string') ? _isoToMs(ps.createdAt) : null;
   383	      if (hsCreatedMs !== null && (refNowMs - hsCreatedMs) > maxAgeMs) {
   384	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.STALE_EVIDENCE], { detail: 'hypothesisSet exceeds max age' });
   385	      }
   386	      if (psCreatedMs !== null && (refNowMs - psCreatedMs) > maxAgeMs) {
   387	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.STALE_EVIDENCE], { detail: 'prioritySet exceeds max age' });
   388	      }
   389	
   390	      // ---- Step 9 — compose brief --------------------------------------------------------------
   391	      // Find primary priority (by primaryActionId).
   392	      var primaryAction = null;
   393	      var secondaryAction = null;
   394	      if (typeof ps.primaryActionId === 'string') {
   395	        for (var pi = 0; pi < ps.priorities.length; pi++) {
   396	          if (ps.priorities[pi].priorityId === ps.primaryActionId) { primaryAction = ps.priorities[pi]; break; }
   397	        }
   398	      }
   399	      if (primaryAction !== null) {
   400	        for (var si = 0; si < ps.priorities.length; si++) {
   401	          var sp = ps.priorities[si];
   402	          if (sp.priorityId === primaryAction.priorityId) continue;
   403	          if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(sp.blockingPrerequisiteIds || [], primaryAction.priorityId) !== -1) continue;
   404	          if (sp.eligibleAsPrimary !== true) continue;
   405	          secondaryAction = sp;
   406	          break;
   407	        }
   408	      }
   409	      // Build brief envelope. Note: the engineer-brief-contract requires:
   410	      //   briefId, identity, primaryIssueI18nKey, primaryIssueParams,
   411	      //   secondaryIssueI18nKey, secondaryIssueParams, evidenceSummary, contradictions,
   412	      //   alternativeExplanations, cannotConcludeReasonCodes, nextValidationAction,
   413	      //   driverExperimentI18nKey, setupExperimentI18nKey, confidence, credibility,
   414	      //   provenance, limitations, schemaVersion.
   415	      // D5 also adds (in a SEPARATE D5-internal extension envelope, NOT inside the
   416	      // engineer-brief-contract-validated `brief` field): sourcePrioritySetId,
   417	      // sourceHypothesisSetId, activation gate, createdAt, schemaVersion.
   418	      var hypById = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   419	      for (var hi = 0; hi < hs.hypotheses.length; hi++) {
   420	        HI.safeDefineDataProperty(hypById, hs.hypotheses[hi].hypothesisId, hs.hypotheses[hi]);
   421	      }
   422	      var primaryHyp = primaryAction !== null ? hypById[primaryAction.hypothesisId] : null;
   423	      var secondaryHyp = secondaryAction !== null ? hypById[secondaryAction.hypothesisId] : null;
   424	
   425	      // Compose evidenceSummary from union of supporting + contradicting nodeIds across all
   426	      // hypotheses (deduped, capped). Use stable order based on D3 emission order.
   427	      var evidenceSummary = [];
   428	      var evSeen = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   429	      for (var hh = 0; hh < hs.hypotheses.length && evidenceSummary.length < ARRAY_CAP; hh++) {
   430	        var hp = hs.hypotheses[hh];
   431	        var supEv = (hp.supportingEvidenceIds && hp.supportingEvidenceIds.length) ? hp.supportingEvidenceIds : [];
   432	        for (var ei2 = 0; ei2 < supEv.length && evidenceSummary.length < ARRAY_CAP; ei2++) {
   433	          var nid = supEv[ei2];
   434	          if (HI.safeHasOwn(evSeen, nid)) continue;
   435	          HI.safeDefineDataProperty(evSeen, nid, true);
   436	          HI.safeArrayPush(evidenceSummary, HI.deepFreeze({
   437	            nodeId: nid,
   438	            i18nKey: 'r3.0d.evidence.supporting',
   439	            params: HI.deepFreeze({ hypothesisId: hp.hypothesisId }),
   440	          }));
   441	        }
   442	      }
   443	
   444	      // Compose contradictions array (visible per directive §10 / engineer-brief-contract).
   445	      var contradictions = [];
   446	      for (var hh2 = 0; hh2 < hs.hypotheses.length && contradictions.length < ARRAY_CAP; hh2++) {
   447	        var hp2 = hs.hypotheses[hh2];
   448	        if (hp2.status !== 'contradicted' || !hp2.contradictingEvidenceIds || hp2.contradictingEvidenceIds.length === 0) continue;
   449	        HI.safeArrayPush(contradictions, HI.deepFreeze({
   450	          hypothesisId: hp2.hypothesisId,
   451	          contradictingEvidenceIds: HI.deepFreeze(HI.safeArraySlice(hp2.contradictingEvidenceIds)),
   452	          i18nKey: hp2.i18nKey,
   453	          params: HI.deepFreeze({ category: hp2.category }),
   454	        }));
   455	      }
   456	
   457	      // Compose alternativeExplanations (union of all hypotheses' alternativeExplanationIds, deduped).
   458	      var alternativeExplanations = [];
   459	      var altSeen = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   460	      for (var hh3 = 0; hh3 < hs.hypotheses.length && alternativeExplanations.length < ARRAY_CAP; hh3++) {
   461	        var hp3 = hs.hypotheses[hh3];
   462	        var altIds = hp3.alternativeExplanationIds ? hp3.alternativeExplanationIds : [];
   463	        for (var ai2 = 0; ai2 < altIds.length && alternativeExplanations.length < ARRAY_CAP; ai2++) {
   464	          var aid = altIds[ai2];
   465	          if (HI.safeHasOwn(altSeen, aid)) continue;
   466	          HI.safeDefineDataProperty(altSeen, aid, true);
   467	          HI.safeArrayPush(alternativeExplanations, HI.deepFreeze({
   468	            alternativeId: aid,
   469	            i18nKey: 'r3.0d.alternative.' + aid,
   470	            params: HI.deepFreeze({ originHypothesisId: hp3.hypothesisId }),
   471	          }));
   472	        }
   473	      }
   474	
   475	      // cannotConcludeReasonCodes (union across hypotheses; deduped).
   476	      var cannotConcludeReasonCodes = [];
   477	      var ccSeen = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   478	      for (var hh4 = 0; hh4 < hs.hypotheses.length && cannotConcludeReasonCodes.length < ARRAY_CAP; hh4++) {
   479	        var hp4 = hs.hypotheses[hh4];
   480	        var ccs = hp4.cannotConcludeReasonCodes ? hp4.cannotConcludeReasonCodes : [];
   481	        for (var cci = 0; cci < ccs.length && cannotConcludeReasonCodes.length < ARRAY_CAP; cci++) {
   482	          var ccode = ccs[cci];
   483	          if (HI.safeHasOwn(ccSeen, ccode)) continue;
   484	          if (!RC.isReasonCode(ccode)) continue;
   485	          HI.safeDefineDataProperty(ccSeen, ccode, true);
   486	          HI.safeArrayPush(cannotConcludeReasonCodes, ccode);
   487	        }
   488	      }
   489	
   490	      // limitations — union of hs.limitations + every hypothesis's limitations.
   491	      var limitations = [];
   492	      var limSeen = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   493	      function _addLim(list) {
   494	        if (!_isPlainArray(list)) return;
   495	        for (var li2 = 0; li2 < list.length && limitations.length < ARRAY_CAP; li2++) {
   496	          var lc = list[li2];
   497	          if (HI.safeHasOwn(limSeen, lc)) continue;
   498	          if (!RC.isReasonCode(lc)) continue;
   499	          HI.safeDefineDataProperty(limSeen, lc, true);
   500	          HI.safeArrayPush(limitations, lc);
   501	        }
   502	      }
   503	      _addLim(hs.limitations);
   504	      for (var hh5 = 0; hh5 < hs.hypotheses.length && limitations.length < ARRAY_CAP; hh5++) {
   505	        _addLim(hs.hypotheses[hh5].limitations);
   506	      }
   507	
   508	      // nextValidationAction — FIRST validationActionIds from primary hypothesis (if any).
   509	      var nextValidationAction = null;
   510	      if (primaryHyp !== null && primaryHyp.validationActionIds && primaryHyp.validationActionIds.length > 0) {
   511	        nextValidationAction = HI.deepFreeze({
   512	          actionId: primaryHyp.validationActionIds[0],
   513	          kind: primaryAction !== null ? primaryAction.kind : 'no_action_required',
   514	          i18nKey: 'r3.0d.action.' + primaryHyp.validationActionIds[0],
   515	        });
   516	      }
   517	
   518	      // setupExperimentI18nKey — only if a setup_experiment priority is eligibleAsPrimary
   519	      // (per directive §10 "unsupported setup actions" forbidden).
   520	      var setupExperimentI18nKey = null;
   521	      for (var spi = 0; spi < ps.priorities.length; spi++) {
   522	        var pp = ps.priorities[spi];
   523	        if (pp.kind === 'setup_experiment' && pp.eligibleAsPrimary === true) {
   524	          setupExperimentI18nKey = 'r3.0d.action.setup_experiment.' + pp.priorityId;
   525	          break;
   526	        }
   527	      }
   528	      var driverExperimentI18nKey = null;
   529	      for (var dpi = 0; dpi < ps.priorities.length; dpi++) {
   530	        var dp = ps.priorities[dpi];
   531	        if (dp.kind === 'driver_experiment' && dp.eligibleAsPrimary === true) {
   532	          driverExperimentI18nKey = 'r3.0d.action.driver_experiment.' + dp.priorityId;
   533	          break;
   534	        }
   535	      }
   536	
   537	      // confidence — D1 contract: { state: 'unresolved' | 'not_computed' } at the brief layer.
   538	      // D5 does NOT echo D3's quantitative confidence into the brief layer (per contract).
   539	      // The D3-internal numeric score lives in the hypothesisSet only.
   540	      var briefConfidence = HI.deepFreeze({ state: 'not_computed' });
   541	      // credibility — primary hypothesis's credibility from CONCLUSION_CREDIBILITY ladder
   542	      // ('Physics' / 'Model' / 'Measured' / 'Derived' / 'Heuristic' / 'Unavailable').
   543	      // D3 emits one of these per hypothesis via _aggregateCredibility; downgrade to
   544	      // 'Heuristic' when no primary.
   545	      var briefCredibility = (primaryHyp !== null && primaryHyp.credibility)
   546	        ? primaryHyp.credibility
   547	        : 'Heuristic';
   548	      // provenance — D5 does NOT echo D3's hypothesis.provenance (which is an OBJECT containing
   549	      // ruleId/ruleVersion/etc — NOT a PROVENANCE enum value). The brief PROVENANCE enum
   550	      // (mirrors R3.0B/R3.0C) is {'synthetic','real','unverified'}. D5 is conservative: it
   551	      // does NOT make a real/synthetic claim about evidence sources at this layer (the source
   552	      // signal is consumed by D2 evidence nodes individually and aggregated into hypotheses).
   553	      // 'unverified' is the safe default — orchestrator/UI layer can attach a derived label
   554	      // based on case-record provenance (which D5 does not have authority over).
   555	      var briefProvenance = 'unverified';
   556	
   557	      var briefIdStr = _briefId(hs.hypothesisSetId, ps.prioritySetId);
   558	
   559	      // SOURCE_IDENTITY_KEYS = ['caseId','sessionId','lapId','sourceId','sourceVersion','freshness']
   560	      // — NO schemaVersion key. Producer attestation lives on the envelope, not on identity.
   561	      var briefIdentity = HI.deepFreeze({
   562	        sourceId: 'r3_0d_engineer_brief',
   563	        sourceVersion: SERVICE_VERSION + '.0',
   564	        caseId: hsCA.caseId,
   565	        sessionId: hsCA.sessionId,
   566	        lapId: hsCA.lapId,
   567	        freshness: (typeof hs.createdAt === 'string') ? hs.createdAt : (resolvedClockIso || ''),
   568	      });
   569	
   570	      // The contract-validated `brief` object — closed key set per engineer-brief-contract.
   571	      // HI.deepFreeze used for the outer wrapper (ambient Object.freeze ban per directive §9);
   572	      // all nested arrays/objects are already individually deep-frozen above.
   573	      var brief = HI.deepFreeze({
   574	        briefId: briefIdStr,
   575	        identity: briefIdentity,
   576	        primaryIssueI18nKey: primaryHyp !== null ? primaryHyp.i18nKey : 'r3.0d.brief.no_primary_action',
   577	        primaryIssueParams: HI.deepFreeze({
   578	          hypothesisId: primaryHyp !== null ? primaryHyp.hypothesisId : '',
   579	          priorityId: primaryAction !== null ? primaryAction.priorityId : '',
   580	        }),
   581	        secondaryIssueI18nKey: secondaryHyp !== null ? secondaryHyp.i18nKey : null,
   582	        secondaryIssueParams: secondaryHyp !== null ? HI.deepFreeze({
   583	          hypothesisId: secondaryHyp.hypothesisId,
   584	          priorityId: secondaryAction.priorityId,
   585	        }) : null,
   586	        evidenceSummary: HI.deepFreeze(evidenceSummary),
   587	        contradictions: HI.deepFreeze(contradictions),
   588	        alternativeExplanations: HI.deepFreeze(alternativeExplanations),
   589	        cannotConcludeReasonCodes: HI.deepFreeze(cannotConcludeReasonCodes),
   590	        nextValidationAction: nextValidationAction,
   591	        driverExperimentI18nKey: driverExperimentI18nKey,
   592	        setupExperimentI18nKey: setupExperimentI18nKey,
   593	        confidence: briefConfidence,
   594	        credibility: briefCredibility,
   595	        provenance: briefProvenance,
   596	        limitations: HI.deepFreeze(limitations),
   597	        schemaVersion: 1,
   598	      });
   599	
   600	      // ---- Step 10 — validate brief shape via D1 engineer-brief-contract ----------------------
   601	      var briefCheck = EBC.validateEngineerBriefShape(brief);
   602	      if (briefCheck.valid !== true) {
   603	        return RC.buildBlockedResult(
   604	          briefCheck.reasonCodes ? HI.safeArraySlice(briefCheck.reasonCodes) : [CODES.BRIEF_INVALID],
   605	          { detail: 'engineer-brief-contract validation failed' }
   606	        );
   607	      }
   608	
   609	      // ---- Step 11 — activation gate signal ---------------------------------------------------
   610	      // D5 EXPOSES the gating signal as a derived boolean. The UI host wiring code (separate
   611	      // module / index.html script wiring) consumes this to decide whether to mount the
   612	      // R3.0D UI shell. D5 does NOT touch feature-registry.js or state.json from inside this
   613	      // module — the gate is declarative.
   614	      // Ambient Array.prototype.every ban (§9) — use a captured for-loop.
   615	      var allHypothesesContradicted = false;
   616	      if (hs.hypotheses.length > 0) {
   617	        allHypothesesContradicted = true;
   618	        for (var hac = 0; hac < hs.hypotheses.length; hac++) {
   619	          if (hs.hypotheses[hac].status !== 'contradicted') { allHypothesesContradicted = false; break; }
   620	        }

     2	 * renderer/js/r3-0d-engineer-viewmodel.js — R3.0D D5 · Engineer Brief ViewModel (PRODUCTION).
     3	 *
     4	 * Authoritative entry: buildEngineerViewModel(envelope, opts)
     5	 *
     6	 * Hard contract (SKYLINE 2026-06-29 R3.0D D5 directive §11):
     7	 *   • Input MUST be a D5 AUTHORITATIVE Engineer Brief envelope. Authority enforced via
     8	 *     EB.verifyAuthoritativeEngineerBrief(envelope) — FIRST read on caller-supplied data;
     9	 *     closure-private WeakSet identity attestation in D5. Forged / cloned / structuredClone /
    10	 *     JSON-round-trip envelopes are rejected before any nested property is touched.
    11	 *   • ViewModel is a RE-PROJECTION — it does NOT recompute confidence, credibility,
    12	 *     priority, eligibility, contradictions, or alternative explanations. It DOES translate
    13	 *     the authoritative envelope into a deep-frozen, UI-safe `viewModel` object expressed in
    14	 *     i18n keys + safe params (no localized strings; UI host attaches locale text).
    15	 *   • UI states (directive §13.1): `unavailable`, `blocked`, `loading`, `available`,
    16	 *     `inconclusive`, `stale-cleared`, `error-sanitized`. ViewModel exposes a derived
    17	 *     `displayState` field consumed by the renderer.
    18	 *   • Privacy invariants: NO raw telemetry, NO file paths, NO machine IDs, NO stack traces
    19	 *     in the viewModel. Reason codes and i18n keys ONLY.
    20	 *   • Locale text is NEVER constructed inside the ViewModel — the renderer reads i18nKey
    21	 *     + params and applies its own locale lookup. The ViewModel may emit a closed set of
    22	 *     `displayHint` enums (e.g., 'blocked' | 'inconclusive' | 'available') so renderer
    23	 *     templates can switch on enum, not on free-text.
    24	 *   • All ambient calls go through R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics (HI). Object.isFrozen captured
    25	 *     at module init. Captured Object.keys via HI.safeOwnKeys.
    26	 *   • D5 producer-attestation pattern is mirrored here: deep-frozen output is registered in
    27	 *     a closure-private WeakSet and `verifyAuthoritativeEngineerViewModel` is exposed for
    28	 *     downstream consumers (Orchestrator + R3.0E / R3.0F + host wiring).
    29	 *   • FORBIDDEN behaviors (directive §11):
    30	 *     - Recompute confidence / credibility / priority / eligibility
    31	 *     - Reorder priorities
    32	 *     - Drop or hide contradictions
    33	 *     - Rewrite cannotConcludeReasonCodes
    34	 *     - Upgrade credibility
    35	 *     - Apply free-text locale strings
    36	 *     - Accept unauthoritative envelopes
    37	 *     - Render arbitrary HTML
    38	 *     - Echo caller-supplied strings to errors verbatim

    48	    try { HI = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/hardened-intrinsics.js'); } catch (e) { HI = null; }
    49	    try { RC = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/reason-codes.js'); } catch (e) { RC = null; }
    50	    try { EB = require('./r3-0d-engineer-brief.js'); } catch (e) { EB = null; }
    51	  }
    52	  if (HI === null && typeof R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics !== 'undefined') HI = R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics;
    53	  if (RC === null && typeof R3_0D_ReasonCodes !== 'undefined') RC = R3_0D_ReasonCodes;
    54	  if (EB === null && typeof R3_0D_EngineerBrief !== 'undefined') EB = R3_0D_EngineerBrief;
    55	
    56	  if (!HI || !RC || !EB) {
    57	    throw new Error('r3-0d-engineer-viewmodel.js: missing one or more required R3.0D dependencies');
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  var CODES = RC.REASON_CODES;
    61	
    62	  // Module-init captures.
    63	  var _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS_FROZEN = Object.isFrozen;
    64	  function _isFrozenSafe(v) { try { return _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS_FROZEN(v) === true; } catch (e) { return false; } }
    65	
    66	  // ---- D5 ViewModel producer-attestation captures (mirror D3/D4/D5-service pattern) ---------
    67	  var _WeakSetCtor = WeakSet;
    68	  var _WS_ADD = WeakSet.prototype.add;
    69	  var _WS_HAS = WeakSet.prototype.has;
    70	  var _CAPTURED_REFLECT_APPLY = Reflect.apply;
    71	  function _wsAdd(set, value) {
    72	    try { _CAPTURED_REFLECT_APPLY(_WS_ADD, set, [value]); return true; }
    73	    catch (e) { return false; }
    74	  }
    75	  function _wsHas(set, value) {
    76	    try { return _CAPTURED_REFLECT_APPLY(_WS_HAS, set, [value]) === true; }
    77	    catch (e) { return false; }
    78	  }
    79	  var _authoritativeViewModels = new _WeakSetCtor();
    80	  function _registerAuthoritativeViewModel(vm) {
    81	    _wsAdd(_authoritativeViewModels, vm);
    82	  }
    83	  function verifyAuthoritativeEngineerViewModel(candidate) {
    84	    try {
    85	      if (candidate === null || typeof candidate !== 'object') return false;
    86	      if (!_wsHas(_authoritativeViewModels, candidate)) return false;
    87	      if (_CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS_FROZEN(candidate) !== true) return false;
    88	      if (candidate.schemaVersion !== VIEWMODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION) return false;
    89	      if (typeof candidate.viewModelId !== 'string') return false;
    90	      if (typeof candidate.sourceBriefId !== 'string') return false;
    91	      return true;
    92	    } catch (e) { return false; }
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  // ---------- Constants -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96	  var VIEWMODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;
    97	  var SERVICE_VERSION = 1;
    98	
    99	  // Closed enum for displayState — UI templates switch on this.
   100	  var DISPLAY_STATE_ALLOWED = HI.deepFreeze([
   101	    'unavailable',
   102	    'blocked',
   103	    'loading',
   104	    'available',
   105	    'inconclusive',
   106	    'stale-cleared',
   107	    'error-sanitized',
   108	  ]);
   109	
   110	  function _isPlainObject(v) {
   111	    try {
   112	      if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return false;
   113	      return HI.safeIsPlainShape(v) === 'plain-object';
   114	    } catch (e) { return false; }
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  function _buildSanitizedError(displayState, reasonCodes) {
   118	    var codes = HI.safeIsArray(reasonCodes) ? HI.safeArraySlice(reasonCodes) : [CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION];
   119	    return HI.deepFreeze({
   120	      valid: false,
   121	      displayState: displayState,
   122	      reasonCodes: HI.deepFreeze(codes),
   123	      // NEVER echo caller content / paths / private IDs. The renderer uses displayState +
   124	      // reasonCodes to pick an i18n key.
   125	      viewModel: null,
   126	    });
   127	  }
   128	
   129	  // ---------- Public API: buildEngineerViewModel ------------------------------------------------
   130	  /**
   131	   * buildEngineerViewModel(envelope, opts) — maps an authoritative D5 EngineerBrief envelope
   132	   * into a deep-frozen, UI-safe viewModel.
   133	   *
   134	   * Authority order:
   135	   *   1. EB.verifyAuthoritativeEngineerBrief(envelope) — FIRST read, identity-only.
   136	   *   2. _isFrozenSafe(envelope.brief) — envelope must carry an already-frozen brief.
   137	   *   3. Compose viewModel by re-projecting brief fields (NO recomputation).
   138	   *   4. Deep-freeze + register.
   139	   */
   140	  function buildEngineerViewModel(envelopeIn, optsIn) {
   141	    try {
   142	      // ---- Step 1 — authority gate (verifier-first ordering) -------------------------------
   143	      if (typeof EB.verifyAuthoritativeEngineerBrief !== 'function'
   144	          || EB.verifyAuthoritativeEngineerBrief(envelopeIn) !== true) {
   145	        return _buildSanitizedError('error-sanitized', [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED]);
   146	      }
   147	      var envelope = envelopeIn;
   148	
   149	      // ---- Step 2 — opts shape (only `displayHint` allowed) ---------------------------------
   150	      // displayHint is OPTIONAL — orchestrator can pass 'loading' or 'stale-cleared' to
   151	      // pre-empt the default 'available' / 'inconclusive' derivation.
   152	      var displayHint = null;
   153	      if (optsIn !== undefined && optsIn !== null) {
   154	        if (!_isPlainObject(optsIn)) {
   155	          return _buildSanitizedError('error-sanitized', [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   156	        }
   157	        var optKeys = HI.safeOwnKeys(optsIn);
   158	        for (var i = 0; i < optKeys.length; i++) {
   159	          if (optKeys[i] !== 'displayHint') {
   160	            return _buildSanitizedError('error-sanitized', [CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   161	          }
   162	        }
   163	        if (HI.safeHasOwn(optsIn, 'displayHint')) {
   164	          var dh = optsIn.displayHint;
   165	          if (typeof dh !== 'string' || HI.safeArrayIndexOf(DISPLAY_STATE_ALLOWED, dh) === -1) {
   166	            return _buildSanitizedError('error-sanitized', [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   167	          }
   168	          displayHint = dh;
   169	        }
   170	      }
   171	
   172	      // ---- Step 3 — derive displayState ------------------------------------------------------
   173	      var brief = envelope.brief;
   174	      var activation = envelope.activation;
   175	      var displayState;
   176	      if (displayHint !== null) {
   177	        displayState = displayHint;
   178	      } else if (!activation.uiCapabilityReady) {
   179	        // No primary action / all contradicted / primary blocked → inconclusive UI state.
   180	        displayState = 'inconclusive';
   181	      } else {
   182	        displayState = 'available';
   183	      }
   184	
   185	      // ---- Step 4 — derive viewModelId (deterministic from briefId + displayState) -----------
   186	      var viewModelId = 'vm_' + envelope.briefId + '__' + displayState;
   187	
   188	      // ---- Step 5 — compose viewModel by RE-PROJECTION (NO recomputation) -------------------
   189	      // Every field below is a faithful copy of a brief field. The ViewModel layer adds:
   190	      //   - displayState enum
   191	      //   - viewModelId
   192	      //   - schemaVersion + serviceVersion for downstream consumers
   193	      // NO confidence / credibility / priority is mutated.
   194	      var viewModel = HI.deepFreeze({
   195	        schemaVersion: VIEWMODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   196	        serviceVersion: SERVICE_VERSION,
   197	        viewModelId: viewModelId,
   198	        sourceBriefId: envelope.briefId,
   199	        sourceHypothesisSetId: envelope.sourceHypothesisSetId,
   200	        sourcePrioritySetId: envelope.sourcePrioritySetId,
   201	        displayState: displayState,
   202	        // Re-projected brief fields.
   203	        identity: brief.identity,
   204	        primaryIssue: HI.deepFreeze({
   205	          i18nKey: brief.primaryIssueI18nKey,
   206	          params: brief.primaryIssueParams,
   207	        }),
   208	        secondaryIssue: (brief.secondaryIssueI18nKey !== null && brief.secondaryIssueI18nKey !== undefined)
   209	          ? HI.deepFreeze({
   210	            i18nKey: brief.secondaryIssueI18nKey,
   211	            params: brief.secondaryIssueParams,
   212	          })
   213	          : null,
   214	        evidenceSummary: brief.evidenceSummary,
   215	        contradictions: brief.contradictions,
   216	        alternativeExplanations: brief.alternativeExplanations,
   217	        cannotConcludeReasonCodes: brief.cannotConcludeReasonCodes,
   218	        nextValidationAction: brief.nextValidationAction,
   219	        driverExperiment: (brief.driverExperimentI18nKey !== null && brief.driverExperimentI18nKey !== undefined)
   220	          ? HI.deepFreeze({ i18nKey: brief.driverExperimentI18nKey })
   221	          : null,
   222	        setupExperiment: (brief.setupExperimentI18nKey !== null && brief.setupExperimentI18nKey !== undefined)
   223	          ? HI.deepFreeze({ i18nKey: brief.setupExperimentI18nKey })
   224	          : null,
   225	        confidence: brief.confidence,
   226	        credibility: brief.credibility,
   227	        provenance: brief.provenance,
   228	        limitations: brief.limitations,
   229	        // Activation gate carried through verbatim for the host wiring code.
   230	        activation: activation,
   231	      });
   232	
   233	      // ---- Step 6 — register + return --------------------------------------------------------
   234	      _registerAuthoritativeViewModel(viewModel);
   235	      return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: true, viewModel: viewModel });
   236	
   237	    } catch (eOuter) {
   238	      return _buildSanitizedError('error-sanitized', [CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION]);
   239	    }
   240	  }
   241	
   242	  /**
   243	   * buildBlockedViewModel(reasonCodes, opts) — produce a deep-frozen blocked viewModel WITHOUT
   244	   * an authoritative envelope. Used by the Orchestrator when the upstream D3/D4/D5 pipeline
   245	   * returned a blocked result (e.g., authority verification failed, freshness exhausted).
   246	   *
   247	   * The blocked viewModel carries:
   248	   *   - displayState: 'blocked' | 'unavailable' | 'stale-cleared'
   249	   *   - reasonCodes (sanitized — no path / caller content)
   250	   *   - schemaVersion + viewModelId for parity with the success path
   251	   *
   252	   * It is NOT registered in the authoritative WeakSet (the verifier returns false for blocked
   253	   * viewModels). This lets downstream consumers reliably reject any "blocked" viewModel that
   254	   * tries to masquerade as authoritative.
   255	   */
   256	  function buildBlockedViewModel(displayState, reasonCodes) {
   257	    try {
   258	      if (typeof displayState !== 'string'
   259	          || HI.safeArrayIndexOf(DISPLAY_STATE_ALLOWED, displayState) === -1
   260	          || displayState === 'available' || displayState === 'inconclusive') {
   261	        // Cannot return success-like states from a blocked builder.
   262	        return _buildSanitizedError('error-sanitized', [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   263	      }
   264	      var codes = HI.safeIsArray(reasonCodes) ? HI.safeArraySlice(reasonCodes) : [];
   265	      var sanitized = [];
   266	      for (var i = 0; i < codes.length; i++) {
   267	        if (RC.isReasonCode(codes[i])) HI.safeArrayPush(sanitized, codes[i]);
   268	      }
   269	      var vm = HI.deepFreeze({
   270	        valid: false,
   271	        displayState: displayState,
   272	        reasonCodes: HI.deepFreeze(sanitized),
   273	        viewModel: null,
   274	      });
   275	      return vm;
   276	    } catch (e) {
   277	      return _buildSanitizedError('error-sanitized', [CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION]);
   278	    }
   279	  }
   280	
   281	  // ---------- Public API ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   282	  var api = {
   283	    SERVICE_VERSION: SERVICE_VERSION,
   284	    VIEWMODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION: VIEWMODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   285	    DISPLAY_STATE_ALLOWED: DISPLAY_STATE_ALLOWED,
   286	    buildEngineerViewModel: buildEngineerViewModel,
   287	    buildBlockedViewModel: buildBlockedViewModel,
   288	    verifyAuthoritativeEngineerViewModel: verifyAuthoritativeEngineerViewModel,
   289	  };
   290	  try { HI.deepFreeze(api); } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
   291	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
   292	    module.exports = api;
   293	  } else if (root) {
   294	    root.R3_0D_EngineerViewModel = api;
   295	  }
   296	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);

    11	 *     contradiction status.

   154	    function render(snapshot) {
   155	      try {
   156	        // Reset all sections.
   157	        stateBadge.setAttribute('data-state', snapshot ? snapshot.displayState : 'unavailable');
   158	        stateBadge.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.state.' + (snapshot ? snapshot.displayState : 'unavailable'), {});

   173

   175	        // brief content.
   176	        var ds = snapshot.displayState;
   177	        if (ds !== 'available' && ds !== 'inconclusive') {
   178	          primaryIssueText.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.brief.state.' + ds, {});
   179	          secondaryIssueEl.classList.add('hidden');
   180	          _clearList(contradictionsList);
   181	          _clearList(cannotConcludeList);
   182	          _clearList(limitationsList);
   183	          nextActionText.textContent = '';
   184	          rollbackText.textContent = '';
   185	          stopText.textContent = '';
   186	          credibilityBadge.textContent = '';
   187	          provenanceText.textContent = '';
   188	          return;
   189	        }
   190	
   191	        // For 'available' or 'inconclusive', fetch the AUTHORITATIVE viewmodel reference

   193	        // getCurrentAuthoritativeViewModel to obtain the deep-frozen, attested object).

   196	          // Race window: snapshot says 'available' but viewmodel cleared between events.
   197	          primaryIssueText.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.brief.state.stale-cleared', {});
   198	          return;
   199	        }
   200	
   201	        primaryIssueText.textContent = i18n(vm.primaryIssue.i18nKey, vm.primaryIssue.params || {});
   202	        if (vm.secondaryIssue) {
   203	          secondaryIssueText.textContent = i18n(vm.secondaryIssue.i18nKey, vm.secondaryIssue.params || {});
   204	          secondaryIssueEl.classList.remove('hidden');
   205	        } else {
   206	          secondaryIssueEl.classList.add('hidden');
   207	        }
   208	
   209	        _clearList(contradictionsList);
   210	        for (var ci = 0; ci < vm.contradictions.length; ci++) {
   211	          var c = vm.contradictions[ci];
   212	          var li = doc.createElement('li');
   213	          li.textContent = i18n(c.i18nKey, c.params || {});
   214	          contradictionsList.appendChild(li);
   215	        }
   216	
   217	        _clearList(cannotConcludeList);
   218	        for (var cci = 0; cci < vm.cannotConcludeReasonCodes.length; cci++) {
   219	          var ccLi = doc.createElement('li');
   220	          ccLi.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.reason.' + vm.cannotConcludeReasonCodes[cci], {});
   221	          cannotConcludeList.appendChild(ccLi);
   222	        }

   228	          limitationsList.appendChild(limLi);
   229	        }

   232	          nextActionText.textContent = i18n(vm.nextValidationAction.i18nKey, {});
   233	        } else {
   234	          nextActionText.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.brief.no_next_action', {});
   235	        }
   236	
   237	        // Stop + rollback derive from the next-validation action's i18n family.
   238	        if (vm.nextValidationAction) {
   239	          stopText.textContent = i18n(vm.nextValidationAction.i18nKey + '.stop', {});
   240	          rollbackText.textContent = i18n(vm.nextValidationAction.i18nKey + '.rollback', {});
   241	        } else {
   242	          stopText.textContent = '';
   243	          rollbackText.textContent = '';
   244	        }
   245	
   246	        credibilityBadge.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.credibility.' + vm.credibility, {});
   247	        credibilityBadge.setAttribute('data-credibility', vm.credibility);
   248	        provenanceText.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.provenance.' + vm.provenance, {});
   249	      } catch (e) {
   250	        // Render-only must never throw out — fail closed to a sanitized error state.
   251	        stateBadge.setAttribute('data-state', 'error-sanitized');
   252	        stateBadge.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.state.error-sanitized', {});
   253	        primaryIssueText.textContent = i18n('r3.0d.brief.state.error-sanitized', {});
   254	      }
   255	    }
   256	
   257	    // Initial render with whatever the orchestrator currently has (if anything).
   258	    render(ORC.currentState());
   259	
   260	    // Subscribe.
   261	    var unsub = ORC.subscribe(function (snap) { render(snap); });
   262	
   263	    return {
   264	      unmount: function () {
   265	        try { unsub(); } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
   266	        // Codex D5 R1-02 closure: directive §13.4 forbids ANY innerHTML assignment in the
   267	    // render-only UI surface, even a literal empty string. Clear via descendant removal.
   268	    while (rootEl.firstChild) rootEl.removeChild(rootEl.firstChild);
   269	        rootEl.removeAttribute('data-r3-0d-engineer-brief');
   270	      },
   271	      render: render,
   272	    };
   273	  }

   277	    mountEngineerBriefPane: mountEngineerBriefPane,

   281	  } else if (root) {

   772	  function buildPrioritySet(inputIn, optsIn) {
   773	    try {
   774	      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
   775	        return _buildIntrinsicTamperBlock('intrinsic-tampering detected at entry to buildPrioritySet');
   776	      }
   777	
   778	      // ---- Step 0.5 — input + opts descriptor snapshot ----
   779	      if (!_isPlainObject(inputIn)) {
   780	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'input not plain object' });
   781	      }

   784	      if (inputDescNames === null) {
   785	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'cannot read input own keys' });
   786	      }
   787	      for (var idi = 0; idi < inputDescNames.length; idi++) {
   788	        var idName = inputDescNames[idi];
   789	        if (typeof idName === 'symbol') {
   790	          return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'input has symbol key' });
   791	        }
   792	        var idDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(inputIn, idName);
   793	        if (!idDesc) continue;
   794	        if (!('value' in idDesc) || !idDesc.enumerable) {
   795	          return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'input non-data/non-enumerable own key' });
   796	        }
   797	        HI.safeDefineDataProperty(inputSnap, idName, idDesc.value);
   798	      }
   799	      var allowedInputKeys = ['hypothesisSet', 'generationToken', 'contextVersion'];
   800	      var inputSnapKeys = HI.safeKeys(inputSnap);
   801	      if (inputSnapKeys === null) {
   802	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'cannot read inputSnap own keys' });
   803	      }
   804	      for (var ik = 0; ik < inputSnapKeys.length; ik++) {
   805	        if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(allowedInputKeys, inputSnapKeys[ik]) === -1) {

   807	          // (privacy — a path-like key would leak in the blocked envelope).
   808	          return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'unknown input own key' });
   809	        }

   813	      var clockCb = null;

   817	        }
   818	        var clockDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(optsIn, 'clock');
   819	        if (clockDesc) {
   820	          // Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-02 closure: opts.clock MUST be a data descriptor (not accessor).
   821	          // Accessor descriptors can fire on read; we never read them.
   822	          if (!('value' in clockDesc)) {
   823	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'opts.clock accessor descriptor not allowed' });
   824	          }
   825	          if (!clockDesc.enumerable) {

   832	        var optsData = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   833	        var optsOwnNames = HI.safeOwnKeys(optsIn);
   834	        if (optsOwnNames === null) {
   835	          return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'cannot read opts own keys' });
   836	        }
   837	        for (var oi = 0; oi < optsOwnNames.length; oi++) {
   838	          var on = optsOwnNames[oi];
   839	          if (typeof on === 'symbol') {
   840	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'opts symbol key' });
   841	          }
   842	          var od = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(optsIn, on);
   843	          if (!od) continue;
   844	          if (!od.enumerable) {
   845	            // Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-05 closure: never echo caller-supplied key name in detail.
   846	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'opts non-enumerable own key' });
   847	          }
   848	          if (on === 'clock') continue;  // clock handled above with explicit accessor rejection
   849	          if (!('value' in od)) {
   850	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'opts accessor descriptor not allowed' });
   851	          }
   852	          if (typeof od.value === 'function') {
   853	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'opts function field not allowed' });
   854	          }
   855	          HI.safeDefineDataProperty(optsData, on, od.value);
   856	        }
   857	        optsSnap = HI.safeStructuredClone(optsData);
   858	        if (optsSnap === null) {
   859	          return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'opts data not cloneable' });
   860	        }

     5	
     6	// ====== contracts/r3.0d/hardened-intrinsics.js ======
     7	/**
     8	 * contracts/r3.0d/hardened-intrinsics.js — R3.0D Hardened Intrinsics Toolkit.
     9	 *
    10	 * Single source of truth for closure-captured pristine intrinsic references and the narrow set
    11	 * of safe wrappers that the rest of R3.0D's contract / runtime path is permitted to use.
    12	 *
    13	 * Codex D2 Round 15 architectural convergence (per SKYLINE directive 2026-06-29):
    14	 *
    15	 *   "只要內部不再執行ambient lookup ... 而只使用module-init捕獲、closure-private的pristine
    16	 *    references，攻擊者替換ambient method後自我還原也沒有意義，因為hostile replacement根本不會
    17	 *    被呼叫。"
    18	 *
    19	 * Rounds 1-15 demonstrated that any defence-in-depth that lets validation code call an AMBIENT
    20	 * prototype method (`arr.push`, `Object.keys`, `String(x)`, `Number.isInteger`, …) is defeated by
    21	 * a hostile replacement that self-restores on its own invocation. The integrity guard then sees
    22	 * a clean state at the next checkpoint, but the validator already used the rebound binding.
    23	 *
    24	 * This module is the FOUNDATION layer. Every other R3.0D contract + the renderer must call ONLY
    25	 * the safe wrappers exported here. Direct ambient property access (`Array.isArray`, `obj.length`,
    26	 * `JSON.stringify`, `Object.freeze`, etc.) is FORBIDDEN throughout the validation path.
    27	 *
    28	 * The wrappers themselves capture pristine intrinsic references at module-init time (BEFORE any
    29	 * caller code can run) and invoke them via captured `Reflect.apply`. They NEVER:
    30	 *   - Use `.call`, `.apply`, or `.bind` on captured methods (those go through
    31	 *     Function.prototype.* which is rebindable).
    32	 *   - Use ambient property lookup on the captured object (which would fire prototype-chain
    33	 *     accessors installed after import).
    34	 *   - Fall back to JSON-round-trip for cloning (which invokes hostile toJSON / drops Symbols
    35	 *     non-deterministically).
    36	 *   - Invoke caller toJSON / Proxy traps / accessor descriptors (`.value` reads on a
    37	 *     `_ObjectGetOwnPropertyDescriptor` result avoid `[[Get]]` semantics).
    38	 *
    39	 * Inputs that carry an accessor descriptor, a Symbol-keyed own property, a non-enumerable own
    40	 * property, a non-Object.prototype prototype, an Array subclass, a mutated Array prototype, or
    41	 * a class instance fail-closed at the structural audit (`deepOriginalShapeAudit`).
    42	 *
    43	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics).
    44	 */
